# Source catalogue

This catalogue lists all **1,070 original files**. Source IDs remain stable after organization. File dates, version numbers and extraction methods are evidence aids; they do not establish authority by themselves. See [source precedence](04-source-precedence.md) and [audit coverage and limitations](07-audit-report.md).

The catalogue distinguishes extraction/inventory from visual review. Neither means every native layer, audio statement or archive member was fully verified. The [structured inventory](audit/inventory.json) contains hashes and more metadata; [review coverage](audit/review-coverage.json) records the visual survey and video sampling. Duplicate IDs refer to identical bytes, not a preferred or newer source.

## Main evidence to read first

| Source | Role |
|---|---|
| [S0945](#s0945) | V3 wireflow master, saved February 2025; explicit scope-reduction notes |
| [S0913](#s0913) | Website snapshot named February 2025; product principles and v0/v1/v2 roadmap |
| [S0671](#s0671)–[S0689](#s0689) | Historical developer handoff boards; detailed behaviour subject to V3 cuts |
| [S0618](#s0618), [S0619](#s0619) | Styled V1.9 prototype PDF and native XD |
| [S0893](#s0893) | Abbreviated pitch export, August 2022; business and older roadmap context |
| [S0102](#s0102) | Most complete inspected brand guide; inconsistent colour labels flagged |
| [S1069](#s1069) | May 2026 WIP export; many placeholders, not an approved replacement specification |
| [S0890](#s0890) | Encrypted deck; contents blocked |
| [S0906](#s0906) | May 2020 briefing recording; visual samples only, no complete transcript |

The full Keele report extracted unchanged from S0913 is available [here](reference-extracts/keele-social-media-benefits-and-harms-2021.pdf). The remaining archive contents are indexed in [ZIP members](audit/archive-members.json) and [RAR members](audit/rar-members.json).
